243: 	void OnPollSuccess(string data, int dataSize)
244: 	{
245: 		if (!m_Configured)
246: 		{
247: 			return;
248: 		}
249: 
250: 		m_TickPollCallback = m_Tick;
251: 		m_PollInFlight = false;
252: 
253: 		MCPCommandBatch batch = new MCPCommandBatch();
254: 		JsonSerializer serializer = new JsonSerializer();
255: 		string parseError;
256: 		bool parsed = serializer.ReadFromString(batch, data, parseError);
257: 		if (!parsed)
258: 		{
259: 			Log("poll parse failed size=" + dataSize + " error=" + parseError);
260: 			OnPollFail("parse_failed");
261: 			return;
262: 		}
263: 
264: 		if (!batch.commands)
265: 		{
266: 			Log("poll returned null commands");
267: 			OnPollFail("null_commands");
268: 			return;
269: 		}
270: 
271: 		m_Backoff = 0.0;
272: 
273: 		int count = batch.commands.Count();
274: 		if (count > 0)
275: 		{
276: 			Log("poll commands=" + count + " sent_tick=" + m_TickPollSent + " callback_tick=" + m_TickPollCallback);
277: 		}
278: 
279: 		bool deferFromWorldSpawn = false;
280: 		int i = 0;
281: 		while (i < count)
282: 		{
283: 			MCPCommand command = batch.commands.Get(i);
284: 			if (command && command.cmd == "world_spawn")
285: 			{
286: 				deferFromWorldSpawn = true;
287: 				Log("world_spawn deferred id=" + command.id + " from=poll_callback callback_tick=" + m_TickPollCallback);
288: 			}
289: 
290: 			if (deferFromWorldSpawn)
291: 			{
292: 				QueuePendingOrFail(command);
293: 			}
294: 			else if (i < MAX_DISPATCH_PER_TICK)
295: 			{
296: 				Dispatch(command);
297: 			}
298: 			else
299: 			{
300: 				QueuePendingOrFail(command);
301: 			}
302: 			i = i + 1;
303: 		}
304: 	}

313: 		int dispatched = 0;
314: 		while (m_Pending.Count() > 0 && dispatched < MAX_DISPATCH_PER_TICK)
315: 		{

263: 		return true;
264: 	}

512: 	// Poll shares the results RestContext (same URL). GetRestContext caches by
513: 	// exact string, so a distinct poll base is unreachable, and stripping the
514: 	// trailing slash makes the engine concatenate base+request into a malformed
515: 	// URL (error=7). Isolation of stale polls is identity discard of the
516: 	// abandoned callback, not a second context. AbandonInFlightPoll must not
517: 	// reset() when m_PollCtx == m_Ctx.
518: 	protected string PollContextUrl(string url)
519: 	{
520: 		return url;
521: 	}
522: 
523: 	// reset() only a distinct poll context (restapi.c:133). Shared results
524: 	// context must not reset() here (would cancel in-flight POSTs). Detach the
525: 	// live pointer so the next StartPoll allocates a new callback; keep the
526: 	// abandoned object in m_PollCallbackRefs until its GET completion calls
527: 	// ReleaseCallback. Engine retain of RestCallback passed to GET is unknown,
528: 	// so script-side hold is required either way. An orphan that never
529: 	// completes stays retained (bounded residual leak: one object per abandon).
530: 	protected void AbandonInFlightPoll()
531: 	{
532: 		m_PollCallback = null;
533: 
534: 		if (m_PollCtx && m_PollCtx != m_Ctx)
535: 		{
536: 			m_PollCtx.reset();
537: 			m_PollCtx.SetHeader("application/json");
538: 		}
539: 	}

2136: 	//! Depth-first count of widgets named `name` under `scope`, scope included.
2137: 	//! Stops as soon as two are seen: the resolver only needs 0, 1 or more.
2138: 	protected void CountUiWidgetsNamed(Widget scope, string name, MCPUiNameMatch match)
2139: 	{
2140: 		if (!scope || !match)
2141: 		{
2142: 			return;
2143: 		}
2144: 		if (match.count >= 2)
2145: 		{
2146: 			return;
2147: 		}
2148: 
2149: 		if (scope.GetName() == name)
2150: 		{
2151: 			match.count = match.count + 1;
2152: 			if (match.count == 1)
2153: 			{
2154: 				match.first = scope;
2155: 			}
2156: 			if (match.count >= 2)
2157: 			{
2158: 				return;
2159: 			}
2160: 		}
2161: 
2162: 		Widget child = scope.GetChildren();
2163: 		while (child)
2164: 		{
2165: 			CountUiWidgetsNamed(child, name, match);
2166: 			if (match.count >= 2)
2167: 			{
2168: 				return;
2169: 			}
2170: 			child = child.GetSibling();
2171: 		}
2172: 	}

2462: 	//! Returns whether a handler CONSUMED the click, not whether one was found.
2463: 	//! ScriptedWidgetEventHandler.OnClick reports that in its return value
2464: 	//! (1_core\proto\enwidgets.c:658). handlerName is set as soon as a handler is

2466: 	protected bool InvokeUiClick(Widget target, int mouseButton, out string handlerName)
2467: 	{
2468: 		handlerName = "";
2469: 		if (!GetGame())
2470: 		{
2471: 			return false;
2472: 		}
2473: 
2474: 		Widget cursor = target;
2475: 		while (cursor)
2476: 		{
2477: 			Class scriptInst;
2478: 			cursor.GetScript(scriptInst);
2479: 			ScriptedWidgetEventHandler scriptHandler = ScriptedWidgetEventHandler.Cast(scriptInst);
2480: 			if (scriptHandler)
2481: 			{
2482: 				handlerName = scriptInst.ClassName();
2483: 				return scriptHandler.OnClick(target, 0, 0, mouseButton);
2484: 			}
2485: 			if (scriptInst)
2486: 			{
2487: 				bool scriptConsumed = false;
2488: 				int scriptCalled = g_Game.GameScript.CallFunctionParams(scriptInst, "OnClick", scriptConsumed, new Param4<Widget, int, int, int>(target, 0, 0, mouseButton));
2489: 				if (scriptCalled)
2490: 				{
2491: 					handlerName = scriptInst.ClassName();
2492: 					return scriptConsumed;
2493: 				}
2494: 			}
2495: 
2496: 			Class userInst;
2497: 			cursor.GetUserData(userInst);
2498: 			ScriptedWidgetEventHandler userHandler = ScriptedWidgetEventHandler.Cast(userInst);
2499: 			if (userHandler)
2500: 			{
2501: 				handlerName = userInst.ClassName();
2502: 				return userHandler.OnClick(target, 0, 0, mouseButton);
2503: 			}
2504: 			if (userInst)
2505: 			{
2506: 				bool userConsumed = false;
2507: 				int userCalled = g_Game.GameScript.CallFunctionParams(userInst, "OnClick", userConsumed, new Param4<Widget, int, int, int>(target, 0, 0, mouseButton));
2508: 				if (userCalled)
2509: 				{
2510: 					handlerName = userInst.ClassName();
2511: 					return userConsumed;
2512: 				}
2513: 			}
2514: 
2515: 			cursor = cursor.GetParent();
2516: 		}
2517: 
2518: 		UIManager ui = GetGame().GetUIManager();
2519: 		if (ui)
2520: 		{
2521: 			UIScriptedMenu menu = ui.GetMenu();
2522: 			if (menu)
2523: 			{
2524: 				handlerName = menu.ClassName();
2525: 				return menu.OnClick(target, 0, 0, mouseButton);
2526: 			}
2527: 		}
2528: 
2529: 		return false;
2530: 	}

205: 	static void Capture(CarScript car, float dt)
206: 	{
207: 		if (!s_Active || car != s_Car)
208: 		{
209: 			return;
210: 		}
211: 		if (!car.IsOwner())
212: 		{
213: 			Fail("owner_changed");
214: 			return;
215: 		}
216: 
217: 		s_AccumS = s_AccumS + dt;
218: 		float intervalS = 1.0 / s_SampleHz;
219: 		if (s_Count > 0 && s_AccumS < intervalS)
220: 		{
221: 			return;
222: 		}
223: 		float nowS = GetGame().GetTickTime();
224: 		if (s_Count > 0 && nowS < s_LastSampleS)
225: 		{
226: 			Fail("clock_not_monotonic");
227: 			return;
228: 		}
229: 		if (s_Count > 0 && nowS == s_LastSampleS)
230: 		{
231: 			return;
232: 		}
233: 		if (s_Count > 0)
234: 		{
235: 			s_AccumS = s_AccumS - intervalS;
236: 		}
237: 		else
238: 		{
239: 			s_AccumS = 0.0;
240: 		}
241: 		CaptureNow(car, false);
242: 	}
243: 
244: 	static void CaptureContact(CarScript car, string zoneName, vector localPos, Contact data)
245: 	{
246: 		if (!s_Active || car != s_Car || !data)
247: 		{
248: 			return;
249: 		}
250: 
251: 		s_PendingContactCount = s_PendingContactCount + 1;
252: 		bool wheelZone = zoneName.IndexOf("wheel") >= 0;
253: 		if (!wheelZone)
254: 		{
255: 			wheelZone = zoneName.IndexOf("Wheel") >= 0;
256: 		}
257: 		if (!wheelZone)
258: 		{
259: 			wheelZone = zoneName.IndexOf("WHEEL") >= 0;
260: 		}
261: 		if (wheelZone)
262: 		{
263: 			return;
264: 		}
265: 
266: 		s_PendingBodyContactCount = s_PendingBodyContactCount + 1;
267: 		if (s_PendingBodyContactCount == 1 || data.Impulse > s_PendingBodyContactImpulse)
268: 		{
269: 			s_PendingBodyContactZone = zoneName;
270: 			s_PendingBodyContactImpulse = data.Impulse;
271: 			s_PendingBodyContactLocal = localPos;
272: 			s_PendingBodyContactNormal = data.Normal;
273: 			s_PendingBodyContactPenetration = data.PenetrationDepth;
274: 		}
275: 	}

2376: def _marker_rewound(path: str, lookback_lines: int) -> log_tail.TailMarker:
2377:     """Marker rewound by ``lookback_lines``, reading only the file's tail.
2378: 
2379:     D40: this used to read the file whole. DayZ RPTs reach hundreds of MB in a
2380:     long session, and every ``wait_for(log_matches, lookback_lines>0)`` paid for
2381:     it. ``log_tail`` already caps its own reads at ``MAX_TAIL_BYTES``; this now
2382:     respects the same ceiling. Size comes from ``os.fstat`` on the open handle,
2383:     not from ``stat(path)``: the game is appending to this file while we read it,
2384:     so the size has to describe the bytes we actually took.
2385:     """
2386:     file_path = Path(path)
2387:     with file_path.open("rb") as handle:
2388:         size = os.fstat(handle.fileno()).st_size
2389:         identity = log_tail._file_identity(
2390:             handle, min(log_tail.IDENTITY_PREFIX_BYTES, size)
2391:         )
2392:         read_size = min(size, log_tail.MAX_TAIL_BYTES)
2393:         window_start = size - read_size
2394:         handle.seek(window_start)
2395:         window = handle.read(read_size)
2396:     offset = _offset_before_last_lines_in_window(window, window_start, lookback_lines)
2397:     return log_tail.TailMarker(
2398:         path=str(file_path), offset=offset, size=size, identity=identity
2399:     )

2401: 
2402: def _log_markers_at_end(paths: list[str]) -> dict[str, log_tail.TailMarker]:
2403:     markers: dict[str, log_tail.TailMarker] = {}
2404:     for path in paths:
2405:         try:
2406:             result = log_tail.read_since(path, None)
2407:         except log_tail.LogTailError:
2408:             continue
2409:         markers[path] = result["marker"]
2410:     return markers
2411: 
2412: 
2413: def _log_markers_with_lookback(
2414:     paths: list[str], lookback_lines: int
2415: ) -> dict[str, log_tail.TailMarker]:
2416:     if lookback_lines <= 0:
2417:         return _log_markers_at_end(paths)
2418:     markers: dict[str, log_tail.TailMarker] = {}
2419:     for path in paths:
2420:         try:
2421:             markers[path] = _marker_rewound(path, lookback_lines)
2422:         except (OSError, log_tail.LogTailError):
2423:             continue
2424:     return markers

2426: 
2427: def _new_log_lines(
2428:     paths: list[str], markers: dict[str, log_tail.TailMarker]
2429: ) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, log_tail.TailMarker], dict[str, int]]:
2430:     """New lines since ``markers``, plus how many each file contributed.
2431: 
2432:     A path missing from the returned counts could not be read at all. That is
2433:     the difference between "the file had nothing new" and "the file was never
2434:     opened", and wait_for used to collapse both into silence.
2435:     """
2436:     lines: list[str] = []
2437:     updated = dict(markers)
2438:     counts: dict[str, int] = {}
2439:     for path in paths:
2440:         try:
2441:             result = log_tail.read_since(path, markers.get(path))
2442:         except log_tail.LogTailError:
2443:             continue
2444:         updated[path] = result["marker"]
2445:         lines.extend(result["lines"])
2446:         counts[path] = len(result["lines"])
2447:     return lines, updated, counts

2449: 
2450: def _scan_log_for_pattern(
2451:     path: str, pattern: str, max_bytes: int
2452: ) -> tuple[str | None, int, bool]:
2453:     """First line of ``path`` containing ``pattern``, scanning from byte 0.
2454: 
2455:     Streams in chunks and returns on the first hit, so a whole launch is
2456:     reachable without holding the file in memory. ``read_since`` cannot serve
2457:     this: it keeps the NEWEST ``max_bytes``, which is right for tailing and
2458:     wrong for a line printed at mission start.
2459: 
2460:     Splits on LF and drops a trailing CR, matching what ``read_since`` hands the
2461:     matcher, so one pattern behaves the same on both paths.
2462:     """
2463:     scanned = 0
2464:     consumed = 0
2465:     carry = b""
2466:     truncated = False
2467:     try:
2468:         with open(path, "rb") as handle:
2469:             while True:
2470:                 budget = max_bytes - consumed
2471:                 if budget <= 0:
2472:                     # Measured, not assumed: only truncated if bytes remain.
2473:                     truncated = bool(handle.read(1))
2474:                     break
2475:                 chunk = handle.read(min(_LAUNCH_SCAN_CHUNK_BYTES, budget))
2476:                 if not chunk:
2477:                     break
2478:                 consumed += len(chunk)
2479:                 carry += chunk
2480:                 start = 0
2481:                 while True:
2482:                     end = carry.find(b"\n", start)
2483:                     if end == -1:
2484:                         break
2485:                     line = carry[start:end].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
2486:                     line = line.rstrip("\r")
2487:                     start = end + 1
2488:                     scanned += 1
2489:                     if pattern in line:
2490:                         return line, scanned, False
2491:                 carry = carry[start:]
2492:     except OSError as error:
2493:         raise log_tail.LogTailError("log_unavailable") from error
2494:     if carry:
2495:         line = carry.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").rstrip("\r")
2496:         scanned += 1
2497:         if pattern in line:
2498:             return line, scanned, truncated
2499:     return None, scanned, truncated

3070:     sleep_fn: Callable[[float], Awaitable[None]] | None = None,
3071:     time_fn: Callable[[], float] | None = None,
3072:     poll_interval_s: float = BOX_WAIT_POLL_S,
3073: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
3074:     """Wait until the box is free and this waiter is FIFO head.
3075: 
3076:     Probes under ``tool_lock`` and sleeps outside it, same rule as
3077:     ``wait_for`` / ``ui_dialog``. Does not take a lease and does not
3078:     require the caller to heartbeat.
3079:     """
3080: 
3081:     sleeper = sleep_fn or asyncio.sleep
3082:     clock = time_fn or time.monotonic
3083:     poll = max(float(poll_interval_s), BOX_WAIT_MIN_POLL_S)
3084:     deadline = clock() + float(wait_s)
3085:     ticket: str | None = None
3086:     box = empty_box(occupied=True)
3087:     session_id = str(getattr(getattr(client, "identity", None), "session_id", "") or "")
3088:     try:
3089:         while True:
3090:             async with client.tool_lock:
3091:                 status = await client.session_box_status(wait=True, ticket=ticket)
3092:             if isinstance(status, dict):
3093:                 box = _box_from_status(status)
3094:                 next_ticket = status.get("box_ticket")
3095:                 wait_error = status.get("box_wait_error")
3096:                 if wait_error in {"box_ticket_invalid", "box_wait_cancelled"}:
3097:                     ticket = None
3098:                 elif isinstance(next_ticket, str) and next_ticket:
3099:                     ticket = next_ticket
3100:             else:
3101:                 wait_error = "box_status_invalid"
3102:             if box.get("port_scan_known") is False:
3103:                 # The box reads occupied because the daemon could not read the
3104:                 # UDP socket table; the FIFO does not repair that, so waiting
3105:                 # would only burn the timeout. Say so at once.
3106:                 return {
3107:                     "ok": False,
3108:                     "ticket": ticket,
3109:                     "box": box,
3110:                     "error": "port_scan_unknown",
3111:                 }
3112:             if wait_error == "box_queue_saturated":
3113:                 return {
3114:                     "ok": False,
3115:                     "ticket": ticket,
3116:                     "box": box,
3117:                     "error": "box_queue_saturated",
3118:                 }
3119:             ready = (
3120:                 isinstance(ticket, str)
3121:                 and ticket
3122:                 and wait_error is None
3123:                 and _box_ready_for(box, session_id)
3124:             )
3125:             if ready:
3126:                 async with client.tool_lock:
3127:                     claimed = await client.session_box_status(
3128:                         wait=True, ticket=ticket, claim=True
3129:                     )
3130:                 if isinstance(claimed, dict):
3131:                     box = _box_from_status(claimed)
3132:                 return {"ok": True, "ticket": ticket, "box": box}
3133:             remaining = deadline - clock()
3134:             if remaining <= 0.0:
3135:                 return {"ok": False, "ticket": ticket, "box": box}
3136:             await sleeper(min(poll, remaining))
3137:     except BaseException:
3138:         if ticket:

3883:         self,
3884:     ) -> tuple[str | None, list[dict[str, object]] | None]:
3885:         """Bound UDP ports with holder pid and image, or why the scan is unknown.
3886: 
3887:         No probe wired means the feature is absent (an empty, known scan), not
3888:         an unknown one: daemons and fixtures built without it keep their

3895:             result = self.port_probe()
3896:         except Exception:

3899:             return "port_scan_unknown", None

3901:         if not isinstance(holders, list):
3902:             return "port_scan_unknown", None
3903:         observed: list[dict[str, object]] = []
3904:         for holder in holders:
3905:             if not isinstance(holder, dict):
3906:                 return "port_scan_unknown", None
3907:             port = holder.get("port")
3908:             if (
3909:                 not isinstance(port, int)
3910:                 or isinstance(port, bool)
3911:                 or not 1 <= port <= 65535
3912:             ):
3913:                 return "port_scan_unknown", None
3914:             pid = holder.get("pid")
3915:             if pid is not None and (
3916:                 not isinstance(pid, int) or isinstance(pid, bool) or pid <= 0
3917:             ):
3918:                 return "port_scan_unknown", None
3919:             name = holder.get("name")
3920:             observed.append(
3921:                 {
3922:                     "port": port,
3923:                     "pid": pid,
3924:                     "name": name if isinstance(name, str) else None,
3925:                 }

366: def listener_pid_for_port(port: int) -> int | None:
367:     """PID of the process LISTENING on ``port`` via ``netstat -ano`` (None if none).
368: 
369:     netstat row layout (verified): ``Proto  Local  Foreign  State  PID`` — the PID
370:     is the last whitespace-separated column and the local address ends with ``:port``.
371:     Console bytes are decoded with ``errors='replace'`` so OEM code-page output
372:     cannot raise ``UnicodeDecodeError`` under UTF-8 mode.
373:     """

375:         return None

377:         result = subprocess.run(
378:             ["netstat", "-ano", "-p", "TCP"],
379:             capture_output=True,
380:             timeout=10,

382:     except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
383:         return None
384:     if result.returncode != 0:
385:         return None
386:     return _listener_pid_from_netstat_output(result.stdout or b"", port)

2781:     def _ports_released(self, pids: set[int]) -> str | None:
2782:         """None when no UDP holder carries one of these pids (P-L2.c).
2783: 
2784:         Fail-closed twice over: a table this daemon cannot read is never
2785:         "released", and a pid still listed answers port_still_held instead of
2786:         letting the caller retire a record that the pre-launch probe of
2787:         start_run would then read as a foreign holder. A lifecycle wired
2788:         without a port probe has no table to contradict, and _port_holders
2789:         answers an empty known scan for it.
2790: 
2791:         A3-F4: a bare pid is not an identity, and this runs right after the
2792:         guard confirmed the exit, which is the instant that pid stopped being
2793:         one. The OS is free to hand it to anything, so only a holder that could
2794:         plausibly be the game counts: a DayZ image on any port, or any image on
2795:         a port of the band this project launches in. A recycled pid holding an
2796:         unrelated socket no longer blocks the relaunch of a client it never was.
2797:         """
2798:         if not pids:
2799:             return None
2800:         tries = max(1, int(self._role_release_tries))
2801:         reason = "port_scan_unknown"
2802:         for attempt in range(tries):
2803:             scan_reason, holders = self._port_holders()
2804:             if scan_reason is None and holders is not None:
2805:                 held = any(
2806:                     isinstance(holder.get("pid"), int)
2807:                     and holder["pid"] in pids
2808:                     and (
2809:                         _is_dayz_image(holder.get("name"))
2810:                         or holder["port"] in _DAYZ_PORT_RANGE
2811:                     )
2812:                     for holder in holders
2813:                 )
2814:                 if not held:
2815:                     return None
2816:                 reason = "port_still_held"
2817:             else:
2818:                 reason = scan_reason or "port_scan_unknown"
2819:             if attempt + 1 < tries:
2820:                 time.sleep(self._role_release_interval_s)
2821:         return reason
2822:

FILE tools/dayz_mcp/log_tail.py bytes=7734 sha256=19b675aba11dcb663b7b91dc67f7319845151cb5388362ac31be1a0b71ca5f30
145:     changed (a wipe-and-rewrite keeps the path and can keep the size, so size
146:     alone misses it). The read then restarts at zero instead of resuming at an
147:     offset that now points into unrelated content.
148: 
149:     `max_lines` caps what is RETURNED, and the returned marker advances only to
150:     the end of the lines actually handed over -- capping output while reporting a
151:     marker past the withheld lines would drop them silently on the next call.
152: 
153:     Line terminator is LF; CRLF works because it ends in LF. A file using lone CR
154:     would not be split, which DayZ on Windows does not produce.
155:     """
156:     file_path = Path(path)
157:     try:
158:         with file_path.open("rb") as handle:
159:             size = file_path.stat().st_size
160:             # Compare over the SAME prefix length the marker was built with, so
161:             # an append (which only adds bytes after it) cannot look like a change.
162:             previous = 0
163:             if marker is not None:
164:                 previous = _file_identity(
165:                     handle, min(IDENTITY_PREFIX_BYTES, marker.size)
166:                 )
167:             rotated = marker is not None and (
168:                 size < marker.size
169:                 or size < marker.offset
170:                 or (marker.identity != 0 and previous != marker.identity)
171:             )
172:             start = 0 if (marker is None or rotated) else marker.offset
173:             truncated = False
174:             if size - start > max_bytes:
175:                 # Keep the NEWEST bytes: on a burst the tail diagnoses the run.
176:                 start = size - max_bytes
177:                 truncated = True
178:             handle.seek(start)
179:             payload = handle.read(max_bytes)
180:             new_identity = _file_identity(handle, min(IDENTITY_PREFIX_BYTES, size))
181:     except OSError as error:
182:         raise LogTailError("log_unavailable") from error
183: 
184:     # Split on BYTES, never on decoded text: with errors="replace" an invalid
185:     # byte becomes U+FFFD, which re-encodes to 3 bytes, so measuring the withheld
186:     # tail in characters would desynchronise the offset. A trailing partial line
187:     # is left for next time -- half a line reported as whole is a lie the
188:     # consumer cannot detect.
189:     last_newline = payload.rfind(b"\n")
190:     complete = b"" if last_newline == -1 else payload[: last_newline + 1]
191:     if max_lines is not None:
192:         cut = 0
193:         for _ in range(max_lines):
194:             nxt = complete.find(b"\n", cut)
195:             if nxt == -1:
196:                 break
197:             cut = nxt + 1
198:         if cut < len(complete):
199:             truncated = True
200:         complete = complete[:cut]

FILE tools/dayz_mcp/secure_launcher.py bytes=9594 sha256=d4139911a21e28499e96b4fe7fa9c1cff5b26c7683d5b3a98ff2b07283cd3705
27: class _IncrementalRedactor:
28:     def __init__(self, secrets: Iterable[bytes]) -> None:
29:         self._secrets = tuple(
30:             sorted({value for value in secrets if value}, key=len, reverse=True)
31:         )
32:         self._pending = bytearray()
33:         self._max_secret = max((len(value) for value in self._secrets), default=1)
34: 
35:     def feed(self, chunk: bytes) -> bytes:
36:         self._pending.extend(chunk)
37:         return self._consume(final=False)
38: 
39:     def flush(self) -> bytes:
40:         return self._consume(final=True)
41: 
42:     def _consume(self, *, final: bool) -> bytes:
43:         # pop(0) memmoves the unread tail on every unmatched byte.
44:         # find() on each miss scans the remaining buffer, so a repeated
45:         # secret plus an absent one is O(n^2). Advance one byte on
46:         # mismatch and drop the consumed prefix once. The suffix shorter
47:         # than the longest secret stays in pending so a secret split
48:         # across feed() calls still matches.
49:         pending = self._pending
50:         if not pending:
51:             return b""
52:         output = bytearray()
53:         index = 0
54:         length = len(pending)
55:         secrets = self._secrets
56:         max_secret = self._max_secret
57:         limit = length if final else length - max_secret + 1
58:         while index < limit:
59:             matched = next(
60:                 (
61:                     secret
62:                     for secret in secrets
63:                     if pending.startswith(secret, index)
64:                 ),
65:                 None,
66:             )
67:             if matched is not None:
68:                 index += len(matched)
69:                 output.extend(b"[REDACTED]")
70:             else:
71:                 output.append(pending[index])
72:                 index += 1
73:         if index:
74:             del pending[:index]
75:         return bytes(output)
76: 
77:

1013: def _matches_in_place_overwrite(
1014:     current: bytes,
1015:     desired: bytes,
1016:     source: bytes,
1017: ) -> bool:
1018:     shared = min(len(desired), len(source))
1019:     if len(current) == len(source):
1020:         prefix = 0
1021:         while prefix < shared and current[prefix] == desired[prefix]:
1022:             prefix += 1
1023:         suffix_start = len(source)
1024:         while suffix_start > 0 and current[suffix_start - 1] == source[suffix_start - 1]:
1025:             suffix_start -= 1
1026:         return suffix_start <= prefix
1027:     return (
1028:         len(desired) > len(source)
1029:         and len(source) < len(current) <= len(desired)
1030:         and current == desired[: len(current)]
1031:     )

103:     async def _run(self) -> None:
104:         next_due = self._monotonic() + HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_S
105:         try:
106:             while not self._stop.is_set():
107:                 delay = max(0.0, next_due - self._monotonic())

121:                     self._fail("lease_heartbeat_failed")
122:                     return
123:                 self._validate_heartbeat(response)
124:                 if self._failure is not None:
125:                     return
126:                 next_due += HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_S
127:                 if self._monotonic() >= next_due:

FILE tools/dayz_mcp/daemon.py bytes=65091 sha256=8c8174975abf15940bace317fca24ea834c8e59062555fdd57b570ad32ff2711
870: def install_run_reaper(
871:     lifecycle: Any,
872:     *,
873:     interval_s: float = REAP_INTERVAL_S,
874:     log: Callable[[str], None] = _noop,
875:     stop: "threading.Event | None" = None,
876: ) -> threading.Thread:
877:     """Arm a daemon thread that periodically retires runs whose processes are all
878:     confirmed dead (ProcessLifecycle.reap_dead_runs). Reaps first, then waits, so the
879:     first pass runs immediately — a fresh/restarted daemon clears a crashed run's stale
880:     RUNNING_IDLE record within one tick instead of blocking every start until a manual
881:     admin reconcile. The reaper never terminates a process; it only retires runs with
882:     zero live processes. ``stop`` and ``interval_s`` are injectable for deterministic tests."""
883: 
884:     def _loop() -> None:
885:         while stop is None or not stop.is_set():
886:             try:
887:                 reaped = lifecycle.reap_dead_runs()
888:             except Exception as exc:  # never let a bad pass kill the reaper thread
889:                 log(f"REAPER: pass failed: {exc}")
890:                 reaped = []
891:             if reaped:
892:                 log(f"REAPER: retired dead runs {reaped}")
893:             if stop is not None:
894:                 if stop.wait(interval_s):
895:                     return
896:             else:
897:                 time.sleep(interval_s)
898: 
899:     thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, name="dayz-mcp-run-reaper", daemon=True)
900:     thread.start()

801:     def session_acquire_wait(self, purpose: str, max_wait_s: float = 300.0) -> dict:
802:         operation_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

813:             while True:

5:   exit $Code
6: }

8: function Get-Hash([string]$Value) {
9:   $sha = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
10:   try {
11:     $bytes = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($Value)
12:     return ([BitConverter]::ToString($sha.ComputeHash($bytes))).Replace("-", "").ToLowerInvariant()
13:   } finally {
14:     $sha.Dispose()
15:   }
16: }

18: function Get-IdentityFromProcess([System.Diagnostics.Process]$proc) {
19:   $processId = [int]$proc.Id
20:   $cim = Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "ProcessId=$processId" -ErrorAction Stop
21:   $creation = $proc.StartTime.ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
22:   $path = [string]$proc.Path
23:   $commandLine = [string]$cim.CommandLine
24:   if (-not $creation -or -not $path -or -not $commandLine) {
25:     throw "identity_incomplete"
26:   }
27:   $normalizedPath = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($path).TrimEnd('\').ToLowerInvariant()
28:   $normalizedCommandLine = $commandLine.Trim()
29:   return [ordered]@{
30:     pid = $ProcessId
31:     creation_time_utc = $creation
32:     executable_sha256 = Get-Hash $normalizedPath
33:     command_line_sha256 = Get-Hash $normalizedCommandLine
34:     identity_complete = $true
35:   }
36: }
37: 
38: function Get-Identity([int]$ProcessId) {
39:   $proc = $null
40:   try {
41:     $proc = Get-Process -Id $ProcessId -ErrorAction Stop
42:   } catch {
43:     if ([string]$_.FullyQualifiedErrorId -like "NoProcessFoundForGivenId*") {
44:       Write-Result ([ordered]@{ identity_complete = $false; error = "process_not_found"; pid = $ProcessId }) 4
45:     }
46:     throw
47:   }
48:   try {
49:     return Get-IdentityFromProcess $proc
50:   } finally {
51:     $proc.Dispose()
52:   }
53: }
54: 
55: function Test-ExpectedIdentity([object]$Expected) {

63:   if ([string]$Expected.command_line_sha256 -notmatch '^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$') { return $false }
64:   return $true
65: }

FILE tools/mcp_capture.py bytes=55983 sha256=20294389b4bd79a640eac75e53351571a740b9eb476bf544d31d9b8bd592de61
18: CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 2.5
19: 
20: # --- Inline token budget (Claude Code MCP cap) --------------------------------
21: # Claude Code rejects a single MCP tool result above MAX_MCP_OUTPUT_TOKENS (VERIFIED 2026-06-28:
22: # default 25000, warning at 10000). The cap is on RESULT tokens, not pixels -- raising the client env
23: # var lets a larger inline image through. The server inherits the same env var by process, so it sizes
24: # the budget loop to whatever ceiling the client enforces; the two MUST agree or the client rejects the
25: # whole response (lost capture). TOKEN_SAFETY_MARGIN keeps the loop under the line: it estimates tokens
26: # as base64-chars/CHARS_PER_TOKEN over the image only, while the client counts real tokens over the
27: # full envelope (image + wrapper + meta). Reference inline widths on a 1302x776 JPEG q82 frame:
28: #   25000 -> ~600px (default) . 50000 -> ~860px (2x px) . 75000 -> ~1070px (3x) . 100000 -> ~native
29: TOKEN_SAFETY_MARGIN = 0.92
30: DEFAULT_CLIENT_TOKEN_CAP = 25000
31: 
32: 
33: def client_token_cap() -> int:
34:     """The MAX_MCP_OUTPUT_TOKENS ceiling the Claude Code client enforces on one tool result, read live
35:     from the environment so server and client stay aligned without a rebuild. Falls back to 25000."""
36:     try:
37:         cap = int(str(os.environ.get("MAX_MCP_OUTPUT_TOKENS", "")).strip())
38:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
39:         return DEFAULT_CLIENT_TOKEN_CAP
40:     return cap if cap > 0 else DEFAULT_CLIENT_TOKEN_CAP
41: 
42: 
43: def default_max_tokens() -> int:
44:     """Safe inline budget = client cap * fail-closed margin. The capture default."""
45:     return max(1, int(client_token_cap() * TOKEN_SAFETY_MARGIN))
46: 
47: 
48: def resolve_request_budget(requested: object = None) -> int:
49:     """Clamp a caller-requested inline token budget to the safe cap. requested <= 0 / None / junk ->
50:     the safe cap (best quality that still fits); a request ABOVE the cap is clamped down so the client
51:     never rejects the response; a request below is honored (spend less context on a capture)."""
52:     cap = default_max_tokens()
53:     try:
54:         req = int(requested)
55:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
56:         return cap
57:     return cap if req <= 0 else min(req, cap)
58: 
59: 
60: DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS = default_max_tokens()
61: DEFAULT_FRAME_COUNT = 4
62: DEFAULT_FRAME_INTERVAL_S = 0.12
63: DEFAULT_STABILITY_THRESHOLD = 0.03
64: 
65: # Delivery encoding for the inline ImageContent. The ~25k-token MCP-output ceiling (CONFLICT-1,
66: # Claude Code issue #9152) is a constraint on the base64 PAYLOAD, not on pixels. A photographic
67: # DayZ frame as PNG is the worst possible choice: offline calibration on a real grab
68: # (fase3-evidence-subject.png, native 1302x776) showed PNG only fits ~208 px wide inside 25k tokens,
69: # so the old SCALE_WIDTHS (small=260/full=320) NEVER fit and the budget loop always shrank to ~208 px
70: # — that is why captures looked unreadable. Same grab as JPEG q82 fits ~592 px (q70 ~704 px) inside
71: # the identical budget: ~2.85x linear / ~8x pixels for free. JPEG is the default; PNG stays selectable.
72: DEFAULT_FORMAT = "jpeg"
73: DEFAULT_QUALITY = 82
74: # "tiny"/"small" are HARD width caps for light captures: they never grow with the budget. "full"
75: # means "as wide as the token budget allows" -- a large start width the budget loop below trims down,
76: # so raising MAX_MCP_OUTPUT_TOKENS actually buys resolution (25k JPEG ~600px, 50k ~860px, 100k ~native).
77: # An int scale is its own explicit cap. The budget loop is always the hard ceiling, whatever the start.
78: SCALE_WIDTHS = {
79:     "tiny": 320,
80:     "small": 512,
81:     "full": 8192,
82: }

21: from __future__ import annotations
22: 
23: import ast
24: import unittest
25: from pathlib import Path
26: 
27: TOOLS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
28: UTF8_BOM = b"\xef\xbb\xbf"
29: 
30: # Directories that hold neither shipped code nor audited scripts.
31: _SKIP_DIRS = frozenset({"__pycache__", ".venv-mcp", "node_modules"})
32: 
33: 
34: def _sources() -> list[Path]:
35:     out: list[Path] = []
36:     for path in TOOLS_DIR.rglob("*.py"):
37:         if any(part in _SKIP_DIRS for part in path.parts):
38:             continue
39:         out.append(path)
40:     return sorted(out)
41: 
42: 
43: class SourcesAreStaticallyAnalysableTest(unittest.TestCase):
44:     def test_no_python_source_starts_with_a_utf8_bom(self) -> None:
45:         offenders = [
46:             path.relative_to(TOOLS_DIR).as_posix()
47:             for path in _sources()
48:             if path.read_bytes().startswith(UTF8_BOM)
49:         ]
50:         self.assertEqual(
51:             offenders,
52:             [],
53:             "UTF-8 BOM found. The module will still import, but every ast-based "
54:             "audit will report parse_error instead of reading it. Re-save as "
55:             "UTF-8 without BOM (PowerShell: -Encoding utf8NoBOM).",
56:         )
57: 
58:     def test_every_python_source_parses_the_way_the_audits_read_it(self) -> None:
59:         failures: list[str] = []
60:         for path in _sources():
61:             relative = path.relative_to(TOOLS_DIR).as_posix()
62:             try:
63:                 source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
64:             except (OSError, UnicodeError) as exc:
65:                 failures.append("%s: %s" % (relative, exc))
66:                 continue
67:             try:
68:                 ast.parse(source, filename=str(path))
69:             except SyntaxError as exc:
70:                 failures.append("%s: %s" % (relative, exc.msg))
71:         self.assertEqual(failures, [], "sources the audits cannot parse: %r" % failures)

17: # `dir/file.ext:12`, `file.ext:12-15`, or a dotfile (`.gitignore:15-16`).
18: # A suffix or a leading-dot name is required so `127.0.0.1` does not match.
19: CITE_RE = re.compile(
20:     r"(?P<path>(?:[\w.-]+/)*(?:[\w.-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9]+|\.[\w-]+)):(?P<a>\d+)(?:-(?P<b>\d+))?"
21: )
22: 
23: # Bare `something.c:N` — Enforce vanilla, not shipped in this repo.
24: VANILLA_C = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_]+\.c$")
25: 
26: # Unpublished trees the README must not point a clone at, with or without :N.
27: UNPUBLISHED = (
28:     "HANDOFF.md",
29:     "poc-verdict.json",
30:     "reviews/",
31:     "decisions/",
32: )
33: 
34: 
35: def classify(path: str) -> str:
36:     if VANILLA_C.match(path):
37:         return "vanilla"
38:     return "in-tree"
39: 
40: 
41: def _tracked_lines(repo: Path, rel: str) -> int | None:
42:     """Line count from the index, or None when the path is not tracked.
43: 
44:     Used only when the file is absent from disk: a sparse checkout excludes
45:     tracked paths from the worktree, and a clone still receives them.
46:     """
47:     try:
48:         blob = subprocess.run(
49:             ["git", "show", f"HEAD:{rel}"],
50:             cwd=repo,
51:             capture_output=True,
52:             check=False,
53:         )
54:     except OSError:
55:         return None

327: class ReadmePathLineCitesDocsTest(unittest.TestCase):
328:     """Every path:line cite in README.md must resolve in this tree.
329: 
330:     Bare `file.c:N` (no directory) is a vanilla DayZ script and is listed,
331:     not failed. Unpublished trees (HANDOFF.md, reviews/, decisions/,
332:     poc-verdict.json) must not be mentioned at all.
333:     """
334: 
335:     def test_readme_path_line_cites_resolve_in_tree(self) -> None:
336:         checker = REPO / "tools" / "checks" / "check_readme_cites.py"
337:         if not checker.is_file():
338:             self.skipTest("tools/checks/check_readme_cites.py absent")
339:         spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
340:             "check_readme_cites", checker)
341:         self.assertIsNotNone(spec)
342:         self.assertIsNotNone(spec.loader)
343:         mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
344:         spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
345:         result = mod.check(readme=_doc_path("README.md"), repo=REPO)
346:         self.assertEqual(
347:             result["missing"], [],
348:             f"README cites files a clone does not have: {result['missing']}")
349:         self.assertEqual(
350:             result["short"], [],
351:             f"README cites a line past EOF: {result['short']}")
352:         self.assertEqual(
353:             result["unpublished"], [],
354:             f"README names unpublished paths: {result['unpublished']}")

33:     def setUp(self) -> None:
34:         # Lock semantics need no particular file, so give each run its own
35:         # instead of contending for the in-tree canonical one.
36:         directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
37:         self.addCleanup(directory.cleanup)
38:         self.lock_path = Path(directory.name) / "approved-launchers.lock"
39:         self.lock_path.touch()
40: 
41:     def acquire(self, *, exclusive: bool) -> registry_lock.RegistryLock:
42:         return registry_lock.acquire_registry_lock(
43:             exclusive=exclusive, path=self.lock_path
44:         )
45: 
46:     def test_shared_readers_coexist_and_exclusive_is_fail_fast(self) -> None:
47:         first = self.acquire(exclusive=False)
48:         second = self.acquire(exclusive=False)
49:         try:
50:             with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "launcher_registry_busy"):
51:                 self.acquire(exclusive=True)
52:         finally:
53:             second.close()
54:             first.close()
55: 
56:         with self.acquire(exclusive=True):
57:             with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "launcher_registry_busy"):
58:                 self.acquire(exclusive=False)
59:

67:             launcher_registry.open_approved_launcher("not-installed")

FILE tools/tests/test_messages_contract.py bytes=8913 sha256=fa95f99d3f6cfda935ed16ba760282a9c65609bc37de9edb3bc74343d046770d
22: def _without_comments(source: str) -> str:
23:     """Remove C/Enforce comments while retaining line/character positions."""
24: 
25:     out: list[str] = []
26:     index = 0
27:     state = "code"
28:     while index < len(source):
29:         char = source[index]
30:         nxt = source[index + 1] if index + 1 < len(source) else ""
31:         if state == "code":
32:             if char == "/" and nxt == "/":
33:                 out.extend("  ")
34:                 index += 2
35:                 state = "line"
36:                 continue
37:             if char == "/" and nxt == "*":
38:                 out.extend("  ")
39:                 index += 2
40:                 state = "block"
41:                 continue
42:             if char == '"':
43:                 state = "string"
44:             out.append(char)
45:             index += 1
46:             continue
47:         if state == "line":
48:             if char == "\n":
49:                 out.append(char)
50:                 state = "code"
51:             else:
52:                 out.append(" ")
53:             index += 1
54:             continue
55:         if state == "block":
56:             if char == "*" and nxt == "/":
57:                 out.extend("  ")
58:                 index += 2
59:                 state = "code"
60:             else:
61:                 out.append("\n" if char == "\n" else " ")
62:                 index += 1
63:             continue
64:         # Strings are copied verbatim so a comment marker inside a literal is
65:         # not interpreted as a comment.  Escapes are kept intact.
66:         out.append(char)

133:             isinstance(task_id, str) and bool(task_id),

135:         )

137:         task_ids.append(task_id)

FILE tools/lote_harness/mutation_gate.py bytes=5924 sha256=3802ab6b467483c766699041cbb77aabeccd6c16c9b006809f973e539f0644dd
1: # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
2: """Re-run a reviewer's mutation from the receiving side and check the control now dies.
3: 
4: Usage:
5:   mutation_gate.py --tree <dir> --file <relpath> --old <text|@file> --new <text|@file>
6:                    --module tests.test_x [--module tests.test_y] [--python <exe>]
7: 
8: A round-2 fix for "this mutation survives" is not accepted on the worker's word. The reviewer
9: says a control is blind; the worker says it fixed it; only the receiver re-applying the exact
10: mutation settles it. Measured 2026-09-07 over nine lanes: six deliveries whose gates were green
11: had a surviving mutation, and every one of them was closed by running this from the outside.
12: 
13: Contract, in order:
14:   1. the module set must be GREEN unmutated -- a base that is already red proves nothing;
15:   2. the mutation must match EXACTLY ONCE in the file, or the run aborts untouched;
16:   3. the module set must be RED mutated -- that, and only that, is a live control;
17:   4. the file is restored from the original bytes in a finally block and the restore is
18:      verified by sha256 before exiting, so an exception mid-run cannot leave a tree mutated.
19: 
20: Exit 0 only when the base is green and the mutant is red.
21: """
22: from __future__ import annotations
23: 
24: import argparse
25: import hashlib
26: import pathlib
27: import subprocess
28: import sys
29: 
30: 
31: def _text(value: str) -> str:
32:     """A literal, or the contents of a file when prefixed with @ (for multi-line hunks)."""
33:     if value.startswith("@"):
34:         return pathlib.Path(value[1:]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
35:     return value
36: 
37: 
38: def _run(python: str, cwd: pathlib.Path, modules: list[str]) -> tuple[bool, str]:
39:     rows: list[str] = []
40:     ok = True
41:     for module in modules:
42:         proc = subprocess.run(
43:             [python, "-B", "-m", "unittest", module],
44:             cwd=str(cwd), capture_output=True, text=True,
45:             encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
46:         )
47:         lines = [
48:             line for line in (proc.stdout + proc.stderr).splitlines()
49:             if line.startswith(("Ran ", "OK", "FAILED"))
50:         ]
51:         rows.append(f"{module}: rc={proc.returncode} {' '.join(lines[-2:])}")
52:         ok = ok and proc.returncode == 0
53:     return ok, "\n    ".join(rows)
54: 
55:

307: def _wait_until(
308:     host: SteamRemediationHost,
309:     timeout_s: float,
310:     predicate: Callable[[], bool],
311: ) -> bool:
312:     deadline = host.monotonic() + timeout_s
313:     while True:
314:         passed = predicate()
315:         now = host.monotonic()
316:         if passed and now <= deadline:
317:             return True
318:         if now >= deadline:
319:             return False
320:         host.sleep(min(_POLL_INTERVAL_S, deadline - now))
321: 
322: 
323: def _wait_until_steam_down(
324:     provider: SteamPreflightProvider,
325:     host: SteamRemediationHost,
326: ) -> str:
327:     """Distinguish down / still alive / unreadable. Never treat None as down."""
328: 
329:     deadline = host.monotonic() + _SHUTDOWN_WAIT_S
330:     while True:
331:         live = _safe_live_pids(provider)
332:         if live is None:
333:             return "unknown"
334:         if live == ():
335:             return "down"
336:         now = host.monotonic()
337:         if now >= deadline:
338:             return "alive"
339:         host.sleep(min(_POLL_INTERVAL_S, deadline - now))
340:

361:

363:     cannot restore ActiveProcess still returns ``steam_session_stale``.
364:     An unreadable process list aborts the cycle; a shutdown that never
365:     finishes does not proceed to ``-silent``. If ``-silent`` raises after
366:     a completed shutdown, one retry is attempted; both failures publish
367:     ``steam_left_down``.

376:         )

379:     except Exception:
380:         return _remediation_result(

382:         )

1601: 
1602: def spawn_detached(argv: list[str], *, log: Callable[[str], None] = _noop, cwd: str | None = None) -> int | None:
1603:     """Launch ``argv`` as a detached background process that aims to survive this session.
1604: 
1605:     Returns the child pid, or None on failure. On Windows the child gets its own
1606:     windowless console and process group, and tries to break away from the parent
1607:     Job so Cowork/node closing does not kill it. CREATE_NO_WINDOW, not
1608:     DETACHED_PROCESS and never the two OR'd together, which Windows resolves by
1609:     ignoring CREATE_NO_WINDOW. Under DETACHED_PROCESS there is no console to
1610:     inherit, so the venv redirector -- which creates the real interpreter with
1611:     creationflags 0 -- allocates a private console for it and a conhost child to
1612:     host it, leaving the wrapper outside that console and the daemon its sole
1613:     member: a console close then delivers CTRL_CLOSE_EVENT to the daemon alone.
1614:     Measured in vivo 2026-09-08; the console presented no desktop window, so the
1615:     exposure is a closable console and a stray host process, not a visible window.
1616:     If the job forbids breakaway the child stays job-bound and
1617:     will die when the session's Job Object closes (KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE); multi-session then
1618:     degrades to "one owner at a time" — the next session's client re-spawns the daemon
1619:     into its own job. Whether breakaway succeeds under Cowork is the in-vivo gate; each
1620:     branch is logged so that gate is diagnosable rather than a black box.
1621: 
1622:     The branch is also planted in the child's environment (DAYZ_MCP_DAEMON_SPAWN_MARKER,
1623:     branch plus this pid) because only the parent knows which Popen succeeded, and its
1624:     log goes to a stderr nobody keeps: the child republishes it into the durable audit
1625:     log at startup. The marker feeds a record and nothing else.
1626:     """
1627:     base_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
1628:         "stdin": subprocess.DEVNULL,
1629:         "stdout": subprocess.DEVNULL,
1630:         "stderr": subprocess.DEVNULL,
1631:         "close_fds": True,
1632:         "cwd": cwd,
1633:     }
1634:     if sys.platform != "win32":
1635:         try:
1636:             return subprocess.Popen(
1637:                 argv,
1638:                 start_new_session=True,
1639:                 env=_child_environment(SPAWN_BRANCH_POSIX_NEW_SESSION),
1640:                 **base_kwargs,
1641:             ).pid
1642:         except OSError as exc:
1643:             log(f"SPAWN: failed to launch daemon: {exc}")
1644:             return None
1645: 
1646:     detached = _CREATE_NO_WINDOW | _CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP
1647:     try:
1648:         pid = subprocess.Popen(
1649:             argv,
1650:             creationflags=detached | _CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB,
1651:             env=_child_environment(SPAWN_BRANCH_BREAKAWAY_OK),
1652:             **base_kwargs,
